2026-09-19T02:02:45.4388189Z         Event kinds gave_up / crashed / timed_out send a notification but DO
2026-09-19T02:02:45.4388377Z         NOT delete the subscription. The dispatcher may respawn the task and
2026-09-19T02:02:45.4388553Z         fire the same event kind again (e.g. a worker that crashes, gets
2026-09-19T02:02:45.4388742Z         reclaimed, and crashes a second time); the user must hear about the
2026-09-19T02:02:45.4388924Z         second event too. Subscriptions are removed only when the task hits
2026-09-19T02:02:45.4389177Z         a truly final status (done / archived) — see the comment on
